[EVENT: YEM83290]
Date: 2023-10-25
Country: Yemen | Location: Sanaa
Type: Violence against civilians > Attack | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Police Forces of Yemen (2017-) Houthi - Security and Intelligence Service [State forces]
Actor2: Civilians (Yemen) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 1
Sources: Aid Worker Security Database; Al Masdar; Undisclosed Source; Yemen Data Project; Yemen News Agency (SABA) - IRG [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 25 October 2023, Houthi forces from the Security and Intelligence Service tortured and killed 1 Yemeni aid worker -- security and safety director of INGO Save the Children -- in a prison in Sanaa city (coded to Amanat al Asimah). It is unclear when the torture took place but the aid worker died on 25 October and Houthi authorities refused to conduct an autopsy. The aid worker had been in custody since 9 September.

[EVENT: YEM87829]
Date: 2023-11-16
Country: Yemen | Location: Aden - Judicial Complex
Type: Strategic developments > Arrests | Disorder: Strategic developments
Actor1: Police Forces of Yemen (2022-2026) Presidential Leadership Council - STC Security Belt Forces [State forces]
Actor2: Civilians (Yemen) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Abductees Mothers Association; Al Masdar; HRW; Sam Organization [Other-National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 16 November 2023, STC Security Belt forces arrested 1 human rights lawyer while he was leaving his work at Aden - Judicial Complex (Khur Maksar, Aden), assaulting him during the arrest. He was taken and detained at Al-Nasr Camp for nearly four months. The reason behind the arrest is likely his involvement in a case concerning a detainee who died in custody in June 2023. He was subjected to torture and ill-treatment, including severe beatings, prolonged nighttime interrogations, and solitary confinement. He was transferred to a hospital due to the deterioration of his health condition in March 2024.

On 1 December 2023, protesters held large a Houthi-sponsored demonstration in Al Jabin (Al Jabin, Raymah) in solidarity with the Palestinian people and 'resistance' and in support of the Houthi leadership decisions and to denounce the 'crimes of the Zionist entity' in the context of the latest round of violence in Israel-Palestine. Houthi-affiliated political officials took part in the demonstration.

[EVENT: YEM83895]
Date: 2023-12-01
Country: Yemen | Location: Markaz Bilad At Taam
Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Yemen) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Ansar Allah; Yemen Data Project [Local partner-National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 1 December 2023, protesters held large Houthi-sponsored protest stands in the district centers of Raymah governorate, namely in Al Hadiyah (Al Jafariyah), Ad Dil (As Salafiyah), Markaz Bilad At Taam (Bilad At Taam), Kusmah (Kusmah), and Mazhar (Mazhar) in solidarity with the Palestinian people and 'resistance' and in support of the Houthi leadership decisions and to denounce the 'crimes of the Zionist entity' in the context of the latest round of violence in Israel-Palestine.
